Recent action-chunking techniques mitigate latency but use an open-loop execution window, rendering the system blind to rapid force transients caused by fracture events. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Diffusion Policy Augmented by Fast Trajectory Generation (DPA-FTG). Compared to recent visual-tactile approaches that focus on positional correction, DPA-FTG decouples low-frequency planning from high-frequency force regulation. At the high level ($5$ Hz), a conditional diffusion model predicts a sequence of latent parameters for selecting a strategy from a learned vocabulary of task primitives. At the low level ($60$ Hz), a lightweight, force-conditioned policy acts as a neural impedance controller, modulating execution in real-time to maintain contact stability. We validate our approach on a bimanual battery disassembly task involving the separation of a compliant sheet. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that DPA-FTG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, including Reactive Diffusion Policy (RDP).

</details>


### [143] [Shooting for Contact: Contact-Implicit Multiple Shooting for Dynamic Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03116)
*Sergio A. Esteban,Jason H. K. Siu,Derrick Mach,Junheng Li,Vince Kurtz,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可微分模拟器的接触隐式直接模拟多重打靶（DSMS）框架，将运动学上可行的参考轨迹转化为动力学上可行的全身轨迹，从而加速运动模仿强化学习训练，并支持零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动重定向方法过于注重运动学相似性，忽略了全身动力学、接触一致性和执行器限制，导致生成的参考轨迹难以被强化学习策略复现，尤其在接触丰富的行为上表现不佳。

Method: 将可微分模拟器嵌入非线性规划（NLP）中，通过多重打靶方法直接基于仿真求解，内部求解接触、摩擦、冲击、自碰撞和关节限位，同时施加跟踪、执行器和任务约束，无需预设接触时序或显式接触约束。

Result: 与现有重定向方法相比，DSMS加速了运动模仿RL训练，并产生高成功率和低跟踪误差的策略。在Unitree G1上实现了接触丰富的爬行和180度跳跃转身等任务的零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: DSMS通过接触隐式的直接仿真优化，有效弥合了运动学参考与动力学可行轨迹之间的差距，为接触丰富的全身运动重定向和机器人技能学习提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Motion retargeting approaches often prioritize kinematic similarity over whole-body dynamics, contact consistency, and actuation limits, yielding references that are difficult for reinforcement learning (RL) policies to reproduce, particularly for contact-rich behaviors. We present a contact-implicit, direct simulation-based multiple shooting (DSMS) framework that transforms kinematically feasible references into dynamically feasible whole-body trajectories. By embedding a differentiable simulator within a nonlinear program, DSMS resolves contact, friction, impacts, self-collision, and joint limits internally while enforcing tracking, actuation, and task constraints without prescribing a contact schedule or introducing explicit contact constraints. Compared with existing retargeting methods, DSMS accelerates motion-imitation RL training and yields policies with high success rates and low tracking error. We further demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on the Unitree G1 through command-conditioned contact-rich crawling and a highly dynamic 180-degree jump-turn.

</details>


### [144] [DigitCode: Symbolic Tokenization of Hand Motion by Anatomical Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03127)
*Haoyu Gu,Haotian Lu,Jingrun Du,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DigitCode，一种基于手部Labanotation（HL）符号网格的手部离散符号表示方法。通过沿着手部的解剖层级（骨头、手指、整只手）调整、分组和分层HL字母表，将符号表示的量化误差降低了四分之三。研究发现关键杠杆是解剖单元而非量化器家族，且手指级token可作为无需训练的可编辑句柄，用于修复畸形生成手和重定向到机器人。论文还发布了HandTok可复现测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部表示（如关节角度或MANO参数）虽然精确但缺乏结构：手指无法作为符号被索引或编辑，也无法标记姿势是否解剖学有效。离散符号表示可以提供这种结构，HL已证明其可行性（T×40网格），但一个核心问题尚未解答：符号应覆盖的解剖单元是什么——骨头、手指还是整只手？

Method: 在HL网格基础上，沿着手部的单元层级（bone → finger → whole hand）自适应地分组和分层HL字母表，形成DigitCode统一编码。通过对比不同解剖单元和不同量化器家族（training-free vs learned）的效果，验证解剖单元是影响精度的核心变量。

Result: DigitCode将符号表示的量化误差降低了四分之三（相比HL）。在固定解剖单元下，training-free和learned量化器在重建上可互换；只有向下移动解剖层级才会提升精度。手指级token可作为训练无关、可编辑的句柄，成功用于修复畸形生成手和机器人重定向。

Conclusion: 手部离散符号表示的精度提升关键在于选择合适的解剖单元（尤其手指级），而非量化器家族。手指作为可枚举的真实单元，能提供连续表示无法实现的编辑与下游任务能力。HandTok测试平台使手部tokenizer可按单元一一比较，推动该方向的可复现研究。

Abstract: Hand motion carries the finest-grained information in human activity, yet the representations behind hand generation, understanding, and robot learning are overwhelmingly continuous--joint angles or MANO parameters. These are accurate but unstructured: a finger cannot be indexed or edited as a symbol, and nothing marks a pose as anatomically valid. Discrete symbolic representations supply exactly this structure, and Hand Labanotation (HL) has shown they are feasible for the hand, writing motion as a T x 40 grid of one fixed direction symbol per bone. Building on this grid, we ask the question underneath it: the anatomical unit a symbol should span--bone, finger, or whole hand. DigitCode answers it by adapting, grouping, and layering HL's alphabet along the hand's unit hierarchy within one code, cutting the symbolic representation's quantization error by three quarters. The lever is the unit, not the quantizer family: at a fixed unit, training-free and learned strong quantizers are interchangeable on reconstruction, while moving down the anatomical hierarchy is what shifts accuracy. The hierarchy also tracks what downstream tasks need. Because a finger is a genuine, enumerable unit, one per-finger token doubles as a training-free, editable handle for jobs a continuous representation cannot address--repairing malformed generated hands, and retargeting them onto robots. We release HandTok, a reproducible testbed, so hand tokenizers can be compared unit-for-unit. Project page: https://digitcode-demo.github.io.

</details>


### [145] [POMDPs for Autonomous Science Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03155)
*Daniel Guirguis,Nathan Wallace,Hanna Kurniawati,Salah Sukkarieh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SHM-POMDP框架，通过基于推断的物理属性而非原始传感器数据进行分支，使科学驱动的信念空间规划更加可计算。在50维观测的RockSample域中比连续观测基线提高18.6%奖励并减少32.9%计算时间，在Cuprite高光谱数据上达到信息论基线2.5倍的信息增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主探索任务需要在传感器不确定性和计算约束下做决策，但将科学表征集成到POMDP规划中因高维观测空间而难以实现。信息论规划器通过假设确定性观测来规避这一问题，却牺牲了POMDP提供的原理性不确定性量化。

Method: 提出Science Hypothesis Map POMDP (SHM-POMDP)，核心思路是：不在原始传感器数据上分支，而是对推断出的物理属性进行分支。通过学习的观测模型保留完整传感器信息，同时使规划器能够在不确定性下联合推理导航和科学属性。

Result: 在扩展RockSample域（50维观测）中，SHM-POMDP比连续观测基线提高18.6%的奖励，每步计算时间减少32.9%。在Cuprite高光谱数据的地理探索任务中，比最佳信息论基线获得2.5倍的信息增益，且仅使用均匀先验即可达到oracle性能的80%。

Conclusion: 将层次概率模型集成到信念空间规划中，可以实现可计算且原理性的自主科学探索，在性能上同时超越传统POMDP方法和科学感知的信息论方法。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration missions require decision-making under sensor uncertainty and computational constraints, yet integrating scientific representations into POMDP planning has remained intractable due to high-dimensional observation spaces. Information-theoretic planners overcome this by assuming deterministic observations, sacrificing the principled uncertainty quantification that POMDPs provide. We introduce the Science Hypothesis Map POMDP (SHM-POMDP), which makes science-driven belief-space planning more tractable by branching on inferred physical properties rather than raw sensor data. This preserves full sensor information through learned observation models while enabling the planner to reason jointly about navigation and scientific properties under uncertainty. On an extended RockSample domain with 50-dimensional observations, SHM-POMDP achieves 18.6\% higher rewards and 32.9\% reduced computation time per step than continuous-observation baselines. On realistic geologic exploration using Cuprite hyperspectral data, SHM-POMDP achieves 2.5$\times$ higher information gain than the best information-theoretic baseline by maintaining beliefs and replanning adaptively---reaching 80\% of oracle performance using only uniform priors. These results demonstrate that integrating hierarchical probabilistic models into belief-space planning enables tractable, principled autonomous science that outperforms both traditional POMDP methods and science-aware information-theoretic approaches.

</details>


### [146] [Accelerating Human-Aware Robot Trajectory Generation via Diffusion and Consistency Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03159)
*Byeong-Il Ham,Hyun-Bin Kim,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向人机交互中机器人操作臂的约束运动规划框架，利用RRT/RRT*生成数据集训练扩散模型，并通过一致性蒸馏和关节加权加加速度正则化实现快速、平滑且满足约束的关节空间轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于非冗余机械臂，末端执行器位姿完全指定时，碰撞避免和自碰撞避免等附加要求难以作为零空间次级任务处理，导致在同时考虑安全性和运动学约束的人机交互环境中难以生成可行的关节空间轨迹。

Method: 首先使用RRT和RRT*算法生成碰撞避免和自碰撞避免的轨迹数据集；然后训练扩散模型通过引导采样生成满足约束的轨迹；采用一致性蒸馏减少迭代采样推理时间；在损失函数中加入关节加权加加速度正则化项以惩罚关节加速度突变，促进轨迹平滑。

Result: 仿真结果表明，一致性模型能够在100毫秒内生成150个轨迹候选，保持较高的任务成功率，并且在应用加加速度正则化时显著降低了关节和末端执行器的加加速度。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了非冗余机械臂在HRI环境中同时考虑安全性和运动学约束的轨迹生成难题，在保证实时性和成功率的同时实现了轨迹的平滑性优化。

Abstract: This research proposes a constrained motion planning framework for robot manipulators in human-robot interaction (HRI). For a non-redundant manipulator with a fully specified end-effector pose, additional requirements such as collision avoidance and self-collision avoidance are difficult to handle as simple null-space secondary tasks. This limitation makes it challenging to generate feasible joint-space trajectories in HRI environments where safety and kinematic constraints must be considered simultaneously. To address this limitation, collision- and self-collision-aware trajectories are generated using Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) and RRT* algorithms, and the resulting dataset is used to train a diffusion model that generates constraint-satisfying trajectories through guided sampling. To reduce the inference time required for iterative diffusion sampling, consistency distillation is applied, and a joint-weighted jerk regularization term is incorporated into the loss function to promote smoother trajectories by penalizing abrupt changes in joint acceleration. Simulation results show that the consistency model generates 150 trajectory candidates in less than 100 ms, maintains a high episode success rate, and substantially reduces joint and end-effector jerk when jerk regularization is applied.

</details>


### [147] [PFM-HR: Pose Flow Matching for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03227)
*Yukang Gao,Yi Gu,Yangchen Zhou,Xingyu Chen,Zhaorui Wang,Fanghai Zhang,Hanyang Cao,Zhengyang Shen,Ji Ma,Runhan Zhang,Lei Han,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PFM-HR，一种直接在无顺序的大规模姿态数据上训练的流匹配先验，用于改进人形机器人的物理运动跟踪。引入姿态几何分数（PGS）量化策略生成的动作与先验姿态变化几何的一致性，并用于调节跟踪奖励以引导策略探索，实验证明在单一动作和通用动作跟踪中均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动先验在物理人形跟踪中有效，但时序先验需要有序运动片段，姿态先验对策略引起的姿态转换指导有限，缺乏利用大规模无序姿态数据的方法。

Method: 提出PFM-HR框架：在无序姿态数据上训练流匹配先验；引入姿态几何分数（PGS）量化rollout期间关节坐标变化与先验捕获的姿态变化局部几何的一致性；用PGS调制跟踪奖励引导策略探索结构化姿态变化，先验在各跟踪任务间保持冻结。

Result: 实验表明PFM-HR在单一运动跟踪和通用运动跟踪任务上均优于基线方法，尤其在高度动态运动中改进显著。

Conclusion: PFM-HR证明了基于大规模无序姿态数据训练的流匹配先验可以有效提升物理人形跟踪性能，且跨任务可复用。

Abstract: Motion priors improve reinforcement learning for physics-based humanoid tracking, but temporal priors require ordered motion clips, while pose priors provide limited guidance for policy-induced pose transitions. We present Pose Flow Matching for Humanoid Robots (PFM-HR), a reusable flow matching prior trained directly on large scale unordered pose data. PFM-HR introduces the Pose Geometry Score (PGS), which quantifies how joint coordinate changes during rollouts align with the local geometry of pose variation captured by the prior. Using PGS to modulate the tracking reward guides policy exploration toward structured pose changes while keeping the prior frozen across tracking tasks. Experiments demonstrate that PFM-HR improves both single motion and general motion tracking, especially for highly dynamic motions.

</details>


### [148] [Structure-Aware Robust Fine-Tuning: Defending Vision-Language-Action Robots Against Physical Attention Hijacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03231)
*Jinquan Zhang,Dongfu Yin,Run Yang,Yufeng Yan,Zhen Tian,F. Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了VLA策略在面对物理世界攻击（如可打印对抗补丁）时的脆弱性，提出了一种名为AGSD的攻击方法（通过策略关键的动作-视觉注意力劫持机制）和一种名为SARF的防御方法（结构感知鲁棒微调），在仿真和真实机器人上显著降低了攻击成功率并保持清洁性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略虽然有望实现通用机器人操控，但其在面对物理世界攻击时的鲁棒性极差。作者发现攻击可通过可物理实现的对抗补丁利用动作条件注意力被劫持的机制来可靠地诱导失败，这构成了对机器人安全的严重威胁，亟需针对性研究。

Method: 攻击方法AGSD：基于期望变换(EOT)优化的可打印补丁，同时实现两个目标——(i)将动作-视觉注意力集中到补丁上；(ii)破坏视觉-语言语义对齐，具备跨任务和跨架构的强迁移性。防御方法SARF：零推理开销的鲁棒微调，仅对视觉编码器进行微调，采用特征锚定、策略关键注意力校正和语言引导的几何一致性约束，且限制在语义相关区域内。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，SARF将OpenVLA在AGSD攻击下的失败率从100%降低到14.2%-56.8%（平均28.6%），同时保持清洁环境性能。在真实PiPER机械臂上，平均成功率从23.0%提升到65.0%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，机制层面的鲁棒性（即针对注意力劫持的根本机制进行防御）是保障VLA机器人免受物理注意力劫持攻击的实用路径，为物理世界中VLA策略的安全性提供了新方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies promise general robotic manipulation, but their robustness against physical-world attacks remains fragile. In particular, we show that physically realizable adversarial patches can reliably induce failures by triggering a mechanism we call policy-critical action-to-vision attention hijacking, where action-conditioned attention is diverted from task-relevant regions to a localized patch. To demonstrate the threat, we propose Attention-Guided Semantic Disruption (AGSD), an Expectation-over-Transformation (EOT) optimized printable patch that jointly (i) concentrates action-to-vision attention on the patch and (ii) disrupts vision-language semantic alignment, yielding strong cross-task and cross-architecture transfer. To mitigate such attacks, we introduce Structure-Aware Robust Fine-Tuning (SARF), a zero-inference-overhead defense that fine-tunes only the visual encoder using feature anchoring, policy-critical attention correction, and language-guided geometric consistency restricted to semantically relevant regions. On LIBERO, SARF reduces OpenVLA's failure rate under AGSD from 100% to 14.2%-56.8% (28.6% average) across suites while preserving clean performance, and on a real PiPER manipulator it improves average success under AGSD from 23.0% to 65.0%. These results highlight mechanism-level robustness as a practical path to securing VLA robots against physical attention hijacking.

</details>


### [149] [Learning Context-Aware Motion Priors for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03234)
*Yunyang Mo,Yi Gu,Yangchen Zhou,Hanyang Cao,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了上下文感知运动先验（CMP）框架，通过自适应地将通用运动先验与当前任务上下文对齐，无需手动技能标签或数据集划分，在五个人形控制任务上显著提升了任务性能和样本效率，并保持对不平衡参考分布的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动先验方法通常从整个参考数据集学习通用且与任务无关的先验，在策略训练中统一应用。这样无法区分哪些参考运动与当前任务上下文相关，可能提供无关或冲突的指导信号。

Method: CMP通过两个关键机制实现上下文感知：(1) 利用高优势策略回滚学习上下文-运动兼容性，同时通过演示目标函数使学习到的相关性保持在参考分布内；(2) 将学习到的相关性分数用于重新加权参考监督信号，训练轻量级的上下文条件适配器。

Result: CMP在两种基础先验（对抗运动先验和评分匹配运动先验）上均有效，在五个人形控制任务中一致地提升了任务性能和样本效率，学习了有意义的上下文-运动对齐，并对不平衡的参考分布保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将运动先验适应到任务上下文可以为人类形策略学习提供更相关的指导，CMP框架展示了这种自适应方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Motion priors provide powerful guidance for learning naturalistic humanoid behaviors. However, existing methods typically learn a general, task-agnostic prior from the entire reference dataset and apply it uniformly throughout policy training. As a result, the prior cannot distinguish which reference motions are relevant to the current task context, potentially providing irrelevant or conflicting guidance. We present Context-Aware Motion Priors (CMP), a framework that adapts a general motion prior to the current task context without manual skill labels, dataset partitioning, or a separate skill discovery stage. Specifically, CMP learns context-motion compatibility using high-advantage policy rollouts, while a demonstration-based objective keeps the learned relevance grounded in the reference distribution. The resulting relevance scores reweight reference supervision for training a lightweight context-conditioned adapter. To evaluate the effectiveness and generality of CMP, we instantiate it with both Adversarial Motion Priors and Score-Matching Motion Priors. Across five humanoid control tasks, CMP consistently improves task performance and sample efficiency, learns meaningful context-motion alignment, and remains robust to imbalanced reference distributions. These results show that adapting motion priors to task contexts provides more relevant guidance for humanoid policy learning.

</details>


### [150] [GraspMeanFlow: SE(3)-Equivariant MeanFlow for Few-Step 6-DoF Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03295)
*Jiyong Kwon,Yikun Bai,Amirhossein Mollaali,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GraspMeanFlow，一个SE(3)等变的均值流框架，用于少步数6-DoF抓取姿态生成。通过学习有限时间区间内的平均速度（通过时间排序指数定义），该方法能在单次函数评估下达到迭代SE(3)流模型五步才能接近的EMD性能，同时保持等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SE(3)等变基于流的抓取姿态生成方法虽然能生成与物体旋转平移一致变换的姿态分布，但依赖迭代数值积分，每次抓取需要数十次函数评估，严重限制了实时操作应用。

Method: 1) 提出SE(3)等变MeanFlow框架，学习有限时间区间内的平均速度场，通过时间排序指数精确定义刚体位移；2) 证明点云条件分布经等变平均速度流映射后仍保持不变性；3) 将场条件化为时间对，通过将两个时间提升为等变向量来实现，保持骨干网络不变；4) 训练时结合流匹配边界项与两种一致性项：微分MeanFlow恒等式（需要雅可比-向量积）或等价的半群损失（避免该计算）。

Result: 在ACRONYM数据集上的实验表明：单次函数评估达到迭代SE(3)流模型五步才能接近的EMD；相同框架的第二实例在少步数区将抓取成功率提升最多24.3个百分点；两种方法生成的抓取分布均随物体精确变换。

Conclusion: GraspMeanFlow通过平均速度场设计，在极少步数下实现了高效的6-DoF抓取姿态生成，同时保持SE(3)等变性，显著提升了实时抓取操作的可行性。

Abstract: Recent data-driven methods for synthesizing 6-DoF grasp poses use generative models to learn complex grasp pose distributions and generate diverse candidate poses. In particular, SE(3)-equivariant flow-based models generate grasp poses that transform consistently with object rotations and translations. However, these methods sample by iterative numerical integration, requiring tens of function evaluations per grasp and limiting their use in real-time manipulation. We propose GraspMeanFlow, an SE(3)-equivariant MeanFlow framework for few-step 6-DoF grasp generation. Our method learns the average velocity over a finite time interval, defined through the time-ordered exponential so that it reproduces exactly the rigid-body displacement accumulated over that interval. We prove that a point-cloud-conditioned distribution transported by an equivariant average-velocity flow map remains invariant, so equivariance is retained under few-step sampling, and we condition the field on a pair of times by lifting both to equivariant vectors, leaving the backbone otherwise unchanged. For stable training, we pair a flow-matching boundary term with either of two consistency terms: the differential MeanFlow identity, whose target requires a Jacobian-vector product, or an equivalent semigroup loss that avoids it. Experiments on ACRONYM show that a single function evaluation of GraspMeanFlow reaches the EMD that an iterative SE(3) flow model needs five steps to approach, that a second instantiation of the same framework improves grasp success by up to 24.3 points in the few-step regime, and that both generate grasp distributions transforming exactly with the object.

</details>


### [151] [PLS-Calib: A Partial Least Squares Framework for Event Camera and Odometry Calibration under Ground Motion Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03296)
*Guangyu Li,Xiao Li,Yujie Wu,Changshuo Wang,Prayag Tiwari,Jiang Cai,Fangwen Yu,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PLS-Calib框架，首次利用偏最小二乘（PLS）回归进行地面受限机器人的传感器外参旋转标定，解决了CCA方法中协方差矩阵病态导致数值不稳定的问题，并在事件相机与里程计标定任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转标定方法大多依赖完整6自由度运动，不适用于地面受限机器人；而现有的CCA方法因协方差矩阵病态导致数值不稳定和标定精度不足。

Method: 利用PLS回归对异步异构传感器流之间的潜在运动学相关性进行建模；引入极性感知事件表示以增强圆形标定靶的时空对比度；提出闭式稳定解以规避CCA中的矩阵奇异问题。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，PLS-Calib在标定鲁棒性和精度上均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为受限机器人系统的旋转标定提供了实用且理论扎实的解决方案，并为统计学习技术在神经形态视觉中的应用开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Accurate extrinsic rotation calibration between sensors is fundamental to the performance of robotic perception systems. However, most existing calibration techniques rely on full 6-DoF motion to excite all degrees of freedom, which is often infeasible for ground-constrained robots with limited motion capabilities. Recent approaches designed for such restricted settings, such as Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA)-based methods, suffer from ill-conditioned covariance matrices that lead to numerical instability and suboptimal calibration accuracy. To overcome these limitations, we present a novel rotation calibration framework named PLS-Calib that, for the first time, leverages Partial Least Squares (PLS) regression to model the latent kinematic correlations between asynchronous, heterogeneous sensor streams. Specifically, we apply our method to the calibration of an event camera and an odometry onboard a ground robot. To improve event-based pattern detection, we introduce a polarity-aware event representation, which enhances spatiotemporal contrast in circular calibration targets. Our PLS-based formulation yields a closed-form, stable solution that avoids matrix singularities inherent in CCA-based approaches. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of our approach, demonstrating significant improvements in calibration robustness and accuracy over state-of-the-art methods. This work offers a practical and theoretically grounded solution for rotation calibration in constrained robotic systems and opens up new directions for applying statistical learning techniques in neuromorphic vision.

</details>


### [152] [Shaping Wind-Tunnel Airflow for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles using Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03378)
*Ghadeer Elmkaiel,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线学习算法，用于控制多风扇垂直风洞中的复杂气流场。该方法结合简化物理模型与基于测量的迭代学习，能够高效收敛到期望的气流分布，并成功生成均匀、高斯和抛物线等多种气流剖面，还能为被动滑翔机器人定制气流以大幅提升飞行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进空中机器人的开发与测试需要可控环境中的定制气流剖面，而现有方法难以在复杂气流场中实现高效、准确的控制。

Method: 将简化物理模型与迭代的基于测量的在线学习相结合，通过样本高效的方式收敛到期望的气流分布。

Result: 成功生成均匀、高斯和抛物线等复杂气流剖面，并能生成专为被动滑翔设计的气流以显著增强滑翔机器人飞行性能；在不同风扇数量下均表现出良好的鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线学习算法在多风扇垂直风洞气流控制中兼具高效性、灵活性和鲁棒性，可为空中机器人实验提供可定制的气流环境，尤其在被动滑翔等应用场景中具有显著价值。

Abstract: The development and testing of advanced aerial robots require experiments in controlled environments with tailored airflow profiles. This paper presents an online learning algorithm for controlling the complex airflow field in a multi-fan vertical wind tunnel. Our method combines a simplified physical model with iterative, measurement-based learning, enabling sample-efficient convergence to desired airflow distributions. We demonstrate the method's versatility by generating complex airflow, such as uniform, Gaussian, and parabolic profiles. Crucially, we show that our algorithm can produce an airflow profile specifically designed for passive soaring, greatly enhancing flight performance of a soaring robot. Variability, practical utility, and robustness of our approach are further highlighted by successful operation with a varying number of fans.

</details>


### [153] [RoboReact: Agentic Skill Distillation from Generated Egocentric Videos for Generalizable Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03387)
*Shuliang He,Shuai Wang,Bo Yue,Junchi Teng,Changyu Wang,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboReact是一个从单目RGB-D观测自动合成全身人形机器人操作技能的框架。它通过视频生成模型合成人类操作视频，利用深度感知3D重建提取保持几何关系的交互关键帧，并将其重定向到高自由度人形平台。通过在线物体中心重新对齐和视觉语言模型引导的优化循环来弥合虚拟计划与物理执行之间的差距，最终通过全身控制器实现协调的灵巧操作，无需遥操作或人类示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在人类环境中执行灵巧操作具有巨大潜力，但获取多样化且可泛化的技能成本高昂，主要源于昂贵的硬件数据采集和繁琐的人工标注。虽然视频生成模型可以合成丰富的操作经验，但将这些想象的行为转化为可执行的人形全身技能仍是空白领域。

Method: RoboReact框架包含四个关键步骤：(1) 从单目RGB-D观测生成人类操作视频；(2) 通过深度感知3D重建提取保持几何关系的交互关键帧；(3) 将关键帧重定向到高自由度人形平台并保持手-物交互几何；(4) 通过在线物体中心重新对齐和视觉语言模型引导的优化循环适应几何失配与执行偏差，最终由全身控制器执行优化的技能。

Result: 在真实人形机器人上的实验表明，RoboReact能够在多种物体配置下实现泛化，并能从执行扰动中稳健恢复，整个过程无需遥操作或人类示范。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了生成模型、视觉语言推理和闭环控制相结合的可扩展人形技能获取潜力，为无需昂贵数据采集的自动化人形机器人技能学习提供了新路径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have the potential to perform dexterous manipulation in human environments, yet acquiring diverse and generalizable skills remains costly due to expensive hardware data collection and labor-intensive annotation. Recent advances in video generative models provide a promising opportunity to synthesize rich manipulation experiences from visual observations, but transferring such imagined behaviors into executable whole-body humanoid skills remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present RoboReact, a framework that automatically synthesizes whole-body humanoid manipulation skills from a single egocentric RGB-D observation. RoboReact generates human manipulation videos, extracts geometry-preserving interaction keyframes through depth-aware 3D reconstruction, and retargets them to high-DoF humanoid platforms while preserving hand-object interaction geometry. To bridge the gap between imagined plans and physical execution, RoboReact performs online object-centric re-grounding and leverages a vision-language model-guided refinement loop to adapt skills under geometric mismatch and execution deviations. The refined skills are executed through a whole-body controller, enabling coordinated whole-body manipulation and dexterous interaction. Experiments on real humanoid robots demonstrate that RoboReact generalizes across diverse object configurations and robustly recovers from execution disturbances without requiring teleoperation or human demonstrations. These results highlight the potential of combining generative models, vision-language reasoning, and closed-loop control for scalable humanoid skill acquisition.

</details>


### [154] [Flying over The Uncertain Nature (FORTUNE): Intelligent and Humanistic 3D Path Planning for Low-Altitude Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03408)
*Minghui Liwang,Wenhan Jia,Xinlei Yi,Wenbo Zhu,Yuhan Su,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FORTUNE框架，用于解决低空智能体（多无人机）在动态城市环境中的3D路径规划与任务分配问题，统一建模持续型、时间可预测型和突发型地面兴趣点需求，并考虑噪声暴露和公共安全等社会环境影响，通过离线-在线分层架构实现高效求解，在真实交通数据和合成场景中超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空智能体的普及带来了对城市环境中及时且具有社会责任感的多智能体协同感知的迫切需求，但现有的工作通常假设兴趣点是静态且完全已知的，无法处理异质的时空需求、环境不确定性以及以人为中心的运营约束。

Method: 提出FORTUNE分层离线-在线框架：离线阶段使用Transformer预测Type-II兴趣点的激活时间窗口，并通过增强型麻雀搜索算法（结合优先级感知解码和危险感知进化）生成协调飞行计划；在线阶段使用轻量级精细化模块处理突发的Type-III兴趣点，同时保持全局任务一致性。问题建模为大规模混合整数非线性规划，统一建模三种类型的地面兴趣点需求，并纳入与海拔相关的社会与环境成本。

Result: 在真实交通数据和合成场景上的实验表明，FORTUNE在有效性、可扩展性和实际适用性方面一致优于现有最先进的方法。

Conclusion: FORTUNE通过分层离线-在线架构有效地解决了动态城市环境中多UAV协同感知的复杂问题，兼顾了感知性能与社会合规运营，为低空智能体的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of low-altitude intelligent agents is increasing the demand for timely and socially responsible collaborative sensing in dynamic urban environments. However, jointly addressing heterogeneous spatiotemporal demands, environmental uncertainty, and human-centered operational constraints remains challenging. This paper studies 3D multi-UAV path planning and task assignment under uncertain ground PoI demands. Unlike existing work assuming static and fully known PoIs, we model persistent, temporally predictable, and emergent demands within a unified framework. We further incorporate altitude-dependent societal and environmental costs, including noise exposure and public safety risks, to balance sensing performance with socially compliant operations. To solve the resulting large-scale mixed-integer nonlinear problem, we propose FORTUNE, a hierarchical offline-online framework. Offline, a Transformer predicts Type-II PoI activation windows, while an enhanced sparrow search algorithm generates coordinated flight plans through priority-aware decoding and danger-aware evolution. Online, a lightweight refinement module accommodates emerging Type-III PoIs while preserving global mission coherence. Experiments on real-world traffic data and synthetic scenarios show that FORTUNE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in effectiveness, scalability, and practical applicability.

</details>


### [155] [A Low-Cost Hybrid Reservoir Computing Model for Isolated Sign Language Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03444)
*Nitin Kumar Singh,Arie Rachmad Syulistyo,Yuichiro Tanaka,Hakaru Tamukoh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于轻量级储层计算（RC）的手语识别方法，利用MediaPipe提取关键点，通过混合储层计算（HRC）架构处理，在WLASL100数据集上取得有竞争力的准确率，训练时间仅需数秒，适合边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在手语识别中表现优异，但计算成本高，难以在边缘设备上部署。为了在保持识别性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，作者提出基于储层计算的轻量级手语识别方案。

Method: 首先使用MediaPipe提取身体和手部关键点，捕获手势的空间和时间动态；然后通过混合储层计算（HRC）架构处理关键点，HRC结合了深度储层计算（DRC）和双向储层计算（BRC），将输入转换为高维动态表示；最后使用岭回归模型将HRC最终状态映射到类别标签。

Result: 在WLASL100视频数据集上，该方法取得了Top-1、Top-5和Top-10准确率分别为61.12%、86.05%和92.56%，与深度学习方法的竞争性表现；训练时间大幅缩短至仅数秒，远优于Bi-GRU等深度学习方法的训练时间。

Conclusion: 该储层计算手语识别方法在保持竞争性识别精度的同时具备极低的计算成本，展现出在边缘设备上部署的潜力。

Abstract: Sign language recognition (SLR) enhances communication between hearing and hearing-impaired individuals. Although deep learning (DL) has achieved promising performance in SLR, its high computational cost limits deployment on edge devices. To address this challenge, we propose a lightweight reservoir computing (RC)-based approach for SLR. In the proposed method, MediaPipe extracts body and hand keypoints to capture the spatial and temporal dynamics of gestures. These keypoints are then processed by a hybrid reservoir computing (HRC) architecture that combines deep reservoir computing (DRC) and bidirectional reservoir computing (BRC), transforming the input into a high-dimensional dynamic representation. A ridge regression model maps the final HRC state to class labels. This HRC-based SLR method achieved Top-1, Top-5, and Top-10 accuracies of 61.12%, 86.05%, and 92.56%, respectively, on the Word-Level American Sign Language 100 (WLASL100) video dataset, demonstrating competitive performance compared to deep learning-based approaches. Additionally, due to the lightweight nature of RC, the training time was drastically reduced to only a few seconds compared with DL-based methods such as Bi-GRU.This method offers low computational cost, showing its potential for deployment on edge devices.

</details>


### [156] [Continue or Replan? Bernoulli-Continuation Policy Learning for Adaptive Horizon Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03483)
*Weichen Xu,Zhenhua Liu,Lin Luo,Yaobo Liang,Chengtang Yao,Qingyu Mei,Jian Cao,Xixin Cao,Xing Zhang,Jiaolong Yang,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Bernoulli-Continuation Policy (BCP)，一种轻量级即插即用的自适应执行视界框架，通过连续二元决策（继续或重新规划）替代固定周期重规划，在保持基础VLA模型冻结的情况下，将成功率和执行效率同时提升。在RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及真实机器人任务上均取得显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于分块的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型以固定数量的动作（即执行视界）执行后再重规划，这种与任务进度无关的周期性调度导致关键操作阶段可能基于过期的动作块执行，而非新鲜重规划的结果，限制了任务成功率。

Method: 提出BCP框架：给定固定长度动作块，其续接头将执行视界选择分解为一系列"继续或重规划"的二元决策，引入序数、前缀共享的归纳偏置。由于每个动作块的最优视界不可观测，采用轨迹层面的强化学习训练续接头，并设计重规划效率奖励，同时奖励任务成功和VLA使用效率，防止策略崩溃到不必要地短视界。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上，BCP在13个低成功率任务上平均成功率提升+11.08%，全部50个任务从89.88%提升至93.94%（+4.06%）。可泛化到Randomized设置（+4.06%），迁移到不同基础策略π0.5，在LIBERO上+1.7%，在更难的LIBERO-PRO上+6.8%。真实机器人上，两个操作任务成功率分别从74%升至92%、44%升至84%。开销可忽略，总运行时间低于固定视界基线。

Conclusion: BCP通过将重规划从周期性调度转变为自适应决策，有效解决了VLA模型因固定视界导致的性能瓶颈。其即插即用、基础模型冻结、跨设置跨策略可迁移的特性，使其成为提升VLA任务成功率的高效通用方案。

Abstract: Existing chunk-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models execute a fixed number of actions (i.e., execution horizon) before replanning, turning replanning into a task-agnostic periodic schedule that is independent of task progress. As a result, when no replanning boundary falls before a critical manipulation stage, it is executed from a stale chunk rather than a freshly replanned one. To address this limitation, we propose Bernoulli-Continuation Policy (BCP), a lightweight, plug-and-play framework for adaptive horizon execution that keeps the base VLA frozen. Given a fixed-length action chunk, its continuation head decomposes execution-horizon selection into a sequence of continue-or-replan decisions, which imposes an ordinal, prefix-sharing inductive bias over candidate horizons rather than treating them as independent classes. Since the optimal horizon for each chunk is not observable, we train this head with reinforcement learning from trajectory-level outcomes and introduce a Replanning-Efficiency Reward that jointly rewards task success and efficient VLA usage, discouraging the policy from collapsing to unnecessarily short horizons. On RoboTwin 2.0 with LingBot-VLA as the base policy, BCP improves the average success rate by +11.08% on 13 low-success tasks and from 89.88% to 93.94% (+4.06%) across all 50 tasks. Although trained only under the Clean setting, BCP generalizes to the Randomized setting, raising the average success rate by +4.06%. It also transfers to a different base policy $π_{0.5}$, achieving a better result on LIBERO (+1.7%) and, notably, on the harder LIBERO-PRO (+6.8%). On a real robot, BCP lifts success from 74% to 92% and from 44% to 84% on two manipulation tasks. Meanwhile, its negligible overhead, combined with higher success, makes BCP's overall runtime even lower than the fixed-horizon baselines.

</details>


### [157] [Lightweight 3D Object Detection via Mamba-Based Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03490)
*Quoc Cuong Ninh,Huy Xuan Pham,Anh Tung Nguyen,Dinh Hoan Trinh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于知识蒸馏的3D LiDAR目标检测框架，通过选择性体素空间特征对齐，将教师模型的体素表征转移给学生模型，并利用Mamba（线性时间序列模型）设计多分支教师骨干网络和框感知特征转移机制，在显著降低计算量的同时保持竞争力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR目标检测方法为提升精度而采用复杂架构和大量上下文信息，导致计算成本高，在资源受限的嵌入式设备上性能不佳。需要一种在精度和计算效率之间取得平衡的轻量化检测方案。

Method: （1）提出知识蒸馏框架，实现对象级体素表征的跨网络迁移；（2）利用Mamba设计多分支教师骨干网络；（3）设计框感知特征转移机制，通过基于Mamba的投影模块对齐教师和学生网络中空间对应的体素特征，实现选择性体素空间特征对齐。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实世界数据上的实验表明，该方法在显著降低计算负载的同时，与现有最先进方法相比保持了具有竞争力的检测精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的知识蒸馏框架能够有效平衡3D LiDAR目标检测的精度与计算效率，为资源受限的自驾和机器人感知场景提供了一种可行的轻量化解决方案。

Abstract: 3D object detection using light detection and ranging (LiDAR) sensors requires a balance between accuracy and computational efficiency for onboard perception in autonomous driving and robotic navigation. Many existing LiDAR-based detection methods employ complex architectures to extract features, integrating large amounts of contextual information to enhance accuracy. This often results in significant computational costs, leading to suboptimal performance on resource-constrained embedded devices. In this study, we propose a knowledge distillation framework that transfers object-level voxel representations from a strong teacher model to lightweight student models through selective voxel-space feature alignment. Taking advantage of the linear-time sequence model with selective state spaces (Mamba), we design a multi-branch Mamba teacher backbone and a box-aware feature transfer mechanism that aligns spatially corresponding voxel features between teacher and student networks through a Mamba-based projection module. Experimental results on both a public dataset and real-world data show that our approach significantly reduces computational load while maintaining competitive accuracy compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [158] [Principles of Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03496)
*Daniele Gammelli,Joseph Lorenzetti,Katie Luo,Gioele Zardini,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本书提供了一本关于自主机器人技术的统一入门教材，融合经典机器人学与现代物理AI，基于斯坦福大学数十年教学经验，配套Jupyter notebooks和实操练习，旨在为设计、分析和构建下一代自主系统提供原则性、可访问且面向部署的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人正从研究实验室快速进入日常生活（道路、空中、仓库、太空），机器人自主性已从纯学术追求转变为实际部署中依赖的成熟方法集，需要一本系统化、易于理解且面向实际部署的教材来培养下一代从业者。

Method: 本书在统一的概念框架内整合现代自主技术栈的核心要素，桥接经典机器人学与现代物理AI；每个主要主题均配以Jupyter notebooks和实现驱动的练习，使读者在建立理论理解的同时培养实践直觉。

Result: 成果表现为一本原则性、可访问且面向部署的全面教材，适用于学生、工程师和研究人员，为其进入自主系统这一快速增长领域提供坚实基础。

Conclusion: 该书为进入自主系统领域的学习者和从业者提供了一个兼具理论深度与实践导向的综合资源，是面向现代机器人技术教育的系统性贡献。

Abstract: Autonomous robots are moving rapidly from research labs into everyday life - on roads, in the air, in warehouses, and in space. Robot autonomy is no longer solely an academic pursuit, but a collection of mature, field-tested methods and tools that practitioners rely on in real-world deployments. This book offers a clear, unified introduction to the methods that make this possible. Built on decades of teaching at Stanford, the text develops the core elements of modern autonomy stacks within a single conceptual framework, bridging classical robotics and modern physical AI. Every major topic is paired with hands-on Jupyter notebooks and implementation-driven exercises, so readers build practical intuition alongside theoretical understanding. The result is a principled, accessible, and deployment-aware foundation for anyone seeking to design, analyze, or contribute to the next generation of autonomous systems. This is a comprehensive resource for students, engineers, and researchers entering one of today's fastest-growing fields.

</details>


### [159] [Pivot-Centric Trajectory Prediction: Bridging Long Horizons via Dynamical Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03521)
*Xiucong Zhao,Jindong Tian,Hao Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PCTP（Pivot-Centric Trajectory Prediction），通过引入"枢轴点"将长时程轨迹预测任务分解为不同尺度的短时程子任务，包括枢轴预测和基于枢轴的轨迹细化两个过程，有效解决了长预测视野下引导不足和误差累积的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着预测视野的增长，现有的端点完成法（endpoint-completion）和迭代细化法（iterative-refine）面临引导信号弱和复合误差累积的问题，难以满足自动驾驶对长时程精确运动预测的需求。

Method: PCTP将长时程轨迹预测解耦为两个过程：(1)枢轴预测过程，利用全局地图上下文和智能体间交互来识别轨迹中的关键枢轴点；(2)基于枢轴点的轨迹细化过程，聚焦局部地图细节，基于预测的枢轴点细化短时程轨迹。该方法可灵活集成到大多数现有SOTA轨迹预测模型中。

Result: 在Argoverse I和Argoverse II两个数据集上，PCTP显著提升了领先模型的预测精度，且对模型尺寸影响极小。PCTP结合QCNet在Argoverse II排行榜上超越了所有已发布的非集成方法。

Conclusion: PCTP通过提供更多的中间引导并减少复合误差，有效应对了长时程轨迹预测的挑战，且具有良好的通用性和即插即用特性。

Abstract: Forecasting precise future motion of surrounding agents is essential for reliable autonomous vehicles. However, as the demand for longer prediction horizons increases, existing endpoint-completion or iterative-refine methods increasingly struggle with weak guidance and compounding errors. To tackle the long-horizon prediction challenge, we propose Pivot-Centric Trajectory Prediction (PCTP). By introducing ``pivots'' and focusing on predicting pivot points along extended trajectories, we divide the long-term prediction task into short-term sub-tasks at various scales. Specifically, PCTP decouples the long-term trajectory predicting process into two processes: pivot prediction and pivot-based trajectory refinement. The pivot prediction process aims to utilize global map context and agent-to-agent interactions to identify these ``pivot points'', while the pivot-based trajectory refinement process focuses on local map details and refines the short-term trajectory based on predicted ``pivot points''. Compared with existing methods, PCTP provides more intermediate guidance while reducing compounding errors. Moreover, PCTP is a flexible approach that can be integrated into most state-of-the-art trajectory prediction models. Experimental results show that PCTP improves the prediction accuracy of leading models on both Argoverse I and Argoverse II datasets with minimal impact on model size. Specifically, PCTP combined with QCNet outperforms all published ensemble-free methods on the Argoverse II leaderboard at submission.

</details>


### [160] [Tired Actor: Fatigue-Informed Character Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03528)
*Shengyuan Zhang,Xinpeng Liu,Muchun Niu,Yulong Chen,Lizhuang Ma,Yue Gao,Cewu Lu,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出在物理仿真角色动画中引入疲劳机制（基于Three-Compartment Controller模型），模拟人体有限能量对动作的影响，从而生成更自然、更符合生物力学规律的角色动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理角色动画虽然能模仿广泛动作，但缺乏生物力学和生理学先验，导致动作不自然。传统控制器假设角色拥有无限能量，这与真实人类行为不符。

Method: 采用Three-Compartment Controller (3CC)模型作为疲劳代理，将有限能量限制注入角色动画控制策略中，训练一个能够适应不同疲劳状态进行通用运动模仿的策略。

Result: 通过大量实验分析了疲劳对角色动画自然性、可扩展性和鲁棒性的影响，证明了引入疲劳机制能改善动作质量。

Conclusion: 将行为能量学中的疲劳概念引入物理角色动画是可行且有效的，能为角色控制带来更自然的运动表现，代码将开源。

Abstract: Replicating human behavior with physics simulation has been a long-expected goal in character animation. Existing efforts have achieved impressive performance in imitating a wide span of general motions. However, most existing efforts could still suffer from unnatural movements due to the lack of biomechanical and physiological priors. Given this, we project our sights to advances in behavioral energetics, which demonstrate how energy use shapes human movements. In contrast, current character controllers typically assume the character is equipped with infinite energy over time. Inspired by these, we propose to adopt fatigue as a proxy of the finite energy limit, inject it into general character animation, and thoroughly investigate how fatigue introduces new characteristics to physics-based character control. Leveraging the Three-Compartment Controller (3CC) model, we managed to obtain a policy for general motion imitation under different fatigue statuses. Furthermore, extensive analyses are conducted to demonstrate how fatigue could influence the naturalness, scalability, and robustness of character animation. Our code will be made public.

</details>


### [161] [Human Centric Embodied Intelligence for Soft Wearable Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03556)
*Rainier Natividad,Raye Chen-Hua Yeow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了软体可穿戴机器人的发展现状，提出了以人类为中心的具体智能（HCEI）概念和感知-认知-执行-增强（PCAA）框架，旨在推动该领域从以执行器为先的传统范式转向以感知和认知为驱动的设计方向，最终实现个性化、预测性和以人为中心的可穿戴智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体可穿戴机器人已从概念验证发展为康复、职业辅助和人体增强的实用平台，但领域核心挑战正从材料和执行器转向感知、智能和人类适应的集成。现有研究缺乏统一的框架来整合这些相互依赖的组件，因此需要一个新的概念框架来指导未来研究和跨学科协作。

Method: 提出人类中心具体智能（HCEI）概念，引入感知-认知-执行-增强（PCAA）框架，将感知和认知定位为设计的首要驱动力，超越传统执行器优先范式。基于此框架综述了软材料、可穿戴传感、人工智能、执行、人机交互、数字孪生、临床转化、制造、法规和伦理等多个方面的进展。

Result: 通过PCAA框架系统性地整合了软体可穿戴机器人各领域的相关进展，揭示了感知、认知、执行和人类适应之间如何协同塑造长期个性化和实际应用部署。

Conclusion: 该综述提供了一个统一的概念框架和设计视角，旨在指导未来研究、促进跨学科协作，并加速下一代软体可穿戴机器人向个性化、预测性和以人为中心的可穿戴智能转化。

Abstract: Soft wearable robots have evolved rapidly from proof-of-concept devices into promising platforms for rehabilitation, occupational assistance, and human augmentation. As the field matures, its central challenge extends beyond the development of softer materials and more capable actuators to the integration of sensing, intelligence, and human adaptation into systems that users can wear comfortably, trust, and benefit from over extended periods. This transition motivates the concept of Human-Centric Embodied Intelligence (HCEI), in which intelligence emerges from the coupled human-robot system through the interaction of morphology, multimodal sensing, adaptive cognition, compliant actuation, and the wearer's own physiological and behavioral adaptation. To organize this perspective, this review introduces the Perception-Cognition-Actuation-Augmentation (PCAA) framework, which positions perception and cognition as the primary drivers of design, shifting development beyond the conventional actuator-first paradigm. Using this framework, the review synthesizes advances in soft materials, wearable sensing, artificial intelligence, actuation, human-robot interaction, digital twins, clinical translation, manufacturing, regulation, and ethics, highlighting how these interdependent components collectively shape long-term personalization and real-world deployment. By providing a unified conceptual framework and design perspective, this review aims to guide future research, foster interdisciplinary collaboration, and accelerate the translation of next-generation soft wearable robots toward personalized, predictive, and human-centric wearable intelligence.

</details>


### [162] [Unified Visuomotor Targets: Supervising VLAs Beyond Physical Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03563)
*Zhenyang Feng,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出UVT（统一视觉运动目标），一种联合编码电机控制和视觉场景转变信息的潜在预测目标，无需架构改动和额外数据即可提升VLA模型的训练效率和任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将视觉和语言观测直接映射到机器人动作，但VLM编码的是丰富的高层场景和目标表征，而机器人动作是低层信号且任务结构有限，这种不匹配限制了策略的学习效果和训练效率。

Method: UVT（Unified Visuomotor Target）是一种统一的潜在预测目标，同时编码电机控制信息和视觉场景转变信息，无需修改模型架构或引入额外数据，直接改变策略的预测目标而非模型设计。

Result: 在两个代表性VLA系统上，应用于模拟基准和真实双臂操作任务，UVT显著提升了训练效率、最终任务性能和策略鲁棒性，在有限训练预算和挑战性环境条件下尤其突出。

Conclusion: 通过改变预测目标而非架构设计，可以更有效地弥合VLA模型中高层表征与低层动作之间的差距，为VLA策略训练提供了新的改进方向。

Abstract: VLA models are trained to predict robot actions from visual and language observations. This is a natural choice, but it creates a mismatch: VLMs encode rich, high-level representations of scenes and goals, while robot actions are low-level signals with limited task structure. We ask whether changing what the policy is trained to predict, rather than how it is architecturally designed, can yield better and more efficiently trained policies. We propose UVT (Unified Visuomotor Target), a unified latent prediction target that jointly encodes motor control and visual scene transition information, requiring no architectural changes and no additional data. Applied to two representative VLA systems across simulation benchmarks and real bimanual manipulation tasks, UVT improves training efficiency, final task performance, and policy robustness, with particularly strong gains under limited training budgets and challenging environmental conditions. Rollout videos and additional qualitative results are available at our project webpage: https://unified-visuomotor-targets.github.io/

</details>


### [163] [Active Stiffness Control of a Supportive Continuum Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03677)
*Rana Danesh,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi,Farhad Aghili*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对腱驱动支撑连续体机器人（SCR）提出了一种主动任务空间刚度控制框架，通过投影滑模控制器实现操作臂末端的位姿调节，并利用虚拟笛卡尔弹簧引入主动表观刚度，使系统刚度可在线调整以抵抗外部载荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支撑连续体机器人的被动刚度由机械结构决定，无法在线调整以应对变化的负载或交互力，因此需要主动刚度控制来调节负载响应并维持定位精度；同时闭链结构引入的运动学约束使任务空间调节和刚度控制变得复杂。

Method: 利用已有的几何变量应变模型描述闭链动力学，将动力学投影到约束一致的运动子空间；设计投影滑模控制器在保持约束的同时调节操作臂末端，并通过Lyapunov分析建立闭环稳定性；在位置调节后，基于位置误差反馈引入虚拟笛卡尔弹簧实现主动表观刚度，以塑造力-位移响应。

Result: 在仿真和实验中验证了框架在预设外部载荷和不同期望构型下的有效性。结果表明，增大指令刚度增益可减少负载引起的末端偏移并提高表观方向刚度，从而改善负载抵抗能力和定位鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的主动任务空间刚度控制框架能有效在线调节支撑连续体机器人的刚度，增强其在外部载荷下的定位精度和负载承受能力，为SCR系统的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Supportive continuum robots (SCRs) enhance the load-bearing capability of an operative continuum robot by mechanically coupling it with a supportive arm. However, their passive stiffness is determined by the mechanical configuration and cannot be adjusted online for varying payloads or interaction forces. Active stiffness control is therefore needed to regulate the load response and maintain positioning accuracy. Meanwhile, the closed-chain structure introduces kinematic constraints that complicate task-space regulation and stiffness control. This paper presents an active task-space stiffness control framework for a tendon-driven SCR. An existing geometric variable strain model describes the closed-chain dynamics, which are projected onto the constraint-consistent motion subspace. A projected sliding mode controller regulates the operative arm tip while preserving the constraints, and closed-loop stability is established through Lyapunov analysis. After position regulation, active apparent stiffness is introduced through a virtual Cartesian spring based on position-error feedback to shape the force--displacement response. The framework is evaluated in simulation and experimentally validated under prescribed external loads and different desired configurations. Results show that increasing the commanded stiffness gain reduces load-induced tip deflection and increases apparent directional stiffness, thereby improving load resistance and positioning robustness under external loading.

</details>


### [164] [LiLa-WAM: Lightweight Latent Reasoning World-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03701)
*Fan Yang,Yuting Su,Xiaobo Wang,Yuncheng You,Fugui Fan,Yuting Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,JiaHong Ning,Peiguang Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LiLa-WAM，一种轻量级世界动作模型，在紧凑潜空间中推理未来状态，可在单个24GB GPU上端到端训练，并在RoboTwin、LIBERO和真实机器人任务上取得了优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）计算开销大：像素空间方法在控制无关的视觉细节上投入过多计算资源，而部分潜空间方法需要多阶段训练构建推理空间，导致训练成本高，难以在有限计算预算下进行训练。

Method: 提出LiLa-WAM，核心是设计一个由未来状态预测和动作生成共同塑造的紧凑潜推理空间，保持模型轻量化的同时与控制任务良好对齐。此外提出视觉转换Token（VTT），一种无语言的任务表示方法，将每个任务编码为视觉特征空间中的一个方向。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO和真实机器人任务上验证了有效性，在50个RoboTwin任务上实现90.48%的成功率，且仅需单GPU训练。

Conclusion: LiLa-WAM通过紧凑潜空间推理和VTT任务表示，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持了高性能，证明了轻量级世界动作模型的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: World-action modeling has emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic control, as it empowers models to go beyond reacting to observations and anticipate how a scene will evolve. However, existing WAMs often incur substantial computational overhead. Pixel-space methods often allocate substantial capacity to visual details that may not be directly relevant to control, while some latent-space methods require multi-stage training to construct the reasoning space. The resulting training cost can make such methods difficult to train under modest computational budgets. In this work, we propose LiLa-WAM, a lightweight world-action model that reasons about the future in a compact latent space and can be trained end-to-end on a single 24GB GPU. Its core design is a compact latent reasoning space jointly shaped by future-state prediction and action generation, which keeps the model lightweight while remaining well aligned with control. For task specification, we further propose the Visual Transition Token(VTT), a language-free task representation that encodes each task as a direction in visual feature space. Experiments on RoboTwin~2.0, LIBERO, and real-robot tasks demonstrate LiLa-WAM's effectiveness, achieving 90.48\% success across 50 RoboTwin tasks with single-GPU training.

</details>


### [165] [Track4Action: Distilling World-Centric 3D Tracker into Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03727)
*Chenyi Wang,Xinkai Wang,Bokai Lin,Jialin Tian,Fucheng Zhang,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Track4Action框架，通过将3D世界轨迹跟踪器提取的特征作为特权监督信号，蒸馏到视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略中，使策略能够理解动作如何改变3D世界，在无需部署时使用跟踪器的情况下显著提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作标签只能告诉VLA策略模仿哪些机器人指令，却无法传达这些指令如何改变3D世界。而对齐的演示片段中包含着这一缺失的监督信息——其中的帧转换记录了相应的动作所产生的几何、运动、可见性和相机变化。

Method: Track4Action包含三个核心组件：1) Track4World编码器将演示片段编码为池化的跟踪器特征；2) 可学习的跟踪查询从当前VLA隐藏状态推断该特征，并在共享空间中匹配；3) 通过特征级门控(feature-wise gate)条件化流匹配动作头。训练时使用跟踪器特征作为对齐目标，部署时无需片段或跟踪器。

Result: 在零样本LIBERO-Plus上达到82.3%，比无对齐变体提高7.6个百分点，比LaMP提高3.0个百分点；在RoboTwin 2.0的干净和随机化分割上分别获得80.44%和81.48%；在四个物理双臂任务中平均成功率达67.5%，比无对齐变体高出25.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 在仿真和物理任务上的收益支持了"动作对齐的3D跟踪器特征可作为无需跟踪器的VLA部署的特权监督"这一观点，表明3D世界追踪信息能有效提升VLA策略对世界动态变化的理解与操作能力。

Abstract: Action labels tell a vision-language-action (VLA) policy which robot commands to imitate, but not how those commands change the 3D world. The aligned demonstration clip contains this missing supervision because its $K$ frame transitions record the geometry, motion, visibility, and camera change produced during the corresponding $K$ actions. We introduce Track4Action, a framework that distills this realized transition from a frozen world-centric 3D tracker into a current-observation VLA policy. During training, Track4World encodes the clip $V_{t:t+K}$ into a pooled tracker feature. Learnable track queries infer this feature from current VLA hidden states, match it in a shared space, and condition a flow-matching action head through a feature-wise gate. The tracker feature only defines the alignment target, so neither the clip nor the tracker is used at deployment. Track4Action reaches 82.3% on zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, improving the alignment-free variant by 7.6 points and LaMP by 3.0 points. It obtains 80.44% and 81.48% on the clean and randomized RoboTwin 2.0 splits, and 67.5% average success across four physical bimanual tasks, 25.0 points above the alignment-free variant. The gains across simulation and physical tasks support action-aligned 3D tracker features as privileged supervision for tracker-free VLA deployment. Our project page is available at https://wing0night.github.io/track4action-project-page.

</details>


### [166] [GORDON: Graph-based Object-centric Rewards for Decomposition of Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03753)
*Andrea Protopapa,Davide Buoso,Francesca Pistilli,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GORDON，一种基于图的对象中心奖励学习框架，从无动作的视频演示中学习密集奖励，通过图神经网络将物体和空间关系嵌入任务对齐的潜在空间，并利用活动感知加权池化机制，在长时程任务中自动发现子任务并组合训练策略，在MAGICAL和ManiSkill3基准的七个操作任务上平均成功率74.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作技能强化学习面临奖励设计复杂、稀疏奖励指导有限、人工子任务标注成本高等挑战，而从原始像素学习奖励对视觉变化和背景外观敏感且脆弱。

Method: 将每个视觉场景表示为物体和空间关系的图，用图神经网络以自监督方式将图嵌入任务对齐的潜在空间；引入活动感知加权池化机制突出任务相关物体并掩蔽机器人主导运动；密集奖励通过当前状态到演示目标配置的潜在空间距离计算；通过奖励时间轮廓自动发现子任务，训练子任务特定奖励和策略并顺序组合。

Result: 在MAGICAL和ManiSkill3基准的七个操作任务上，短时程场景下奖励学习得到提升，长时程任务中通过自动分解实现成功策略学习，平均成功率74.4%，比最优学习基线高约35个百分点，比oracle高约25个百分点。

Conclusion: GORDON通过对象中心的图表示和自动子任务分解，有效解决了长时程操作任务中奖励学习与策略训练的问题，在多个基准上显著超越现有基线。

Abstract: Learning long-horizon manipulation skills with reinforcement learning remains challenging due to the complexity of reward design, the limited guidance of sparse rewards, and the high cost of manual subtask annotation. Visual demonstrations can provide supervision for reward learning, but rewards learned from raw pixels can be brittle and sensitive to visual variation, background appearance, and robot motion. In this work, we propose GORDON, a graph-based object-centric reward learning framework that learns dense rewards from action-free video demonstrations. Each visual scene is represented as a graph of detected objects and spatial relations, and a graph neural network is trained in a self-supervised manner to embed these graphs into a task-aligned latent space. To align the representation with semantic task progress, we introduce an activity-aware weighted pooling mechanism that emphasizes task-relevant objects while masking robot-dominated motion. The dense reward is then computed as distances in the learned latent space of the current state to demonstrated goal configurations, providing a measure of task progress. In long-horizon tasks, the temporal profile of this reward reveals stage-wise object-state transitions, enabling automatic subtask discovery without manual segmentation. The discovered segments are then used to train subtask-specific rewards and specialized policies that are composed sequentially. Experiments on seven manipulation tasks on MAGICAL and ManiSkill3 benchmarks show that our object-centric reward improves reinforcement learning in short-horizon settings and enables successful policy learning in complex long-horizon tasks through automatic decomposition, achieving an average success rate of 74.4% across the long-horizon tasks (on average approximately +35 p.p. vs. best learned baseline and approximately +25 p.p. vs. oracle).

</details>


### [167] [Design and Evaluation of an AI-Enabled Cloud-Edge Architecture for Connected Precision Agriculture Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03816)
*Deckshitha Angadi,Koteshwar Goud Surga,Nagaraju Lakkaraju,Naveena Budda,Vikas Agarwal,Giridhara Venkata Ram Raj Mulasa,Ravi Killamsetty,Chandrasekhara Sarma Mallubhotla,Narsimlu Kemsaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI的云边协同架构，融合物联网传感器、无人机、深度学习、Azure IoT Hub云分析和多平台接口，实现对番茄病害的实时自动检测，准确率达到92-95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 番茄作物易受早疫病、晚疫病和叶霉病等病害影响，造成重大产量损失。人工监测仅适用于小规模农场，在大规模场景下难以管理，亟需自动化监测解决方案。

Method: 构建AI驱动的云边架构，集成IoT传感器、无人机、深度学习模型、Azure IoT Hub云分析和多平台界面（移动应用、网页应用和嵌入式边缘设备平台）；使用PlantVillage和Kaggle等公开数据集训练TensorFlow模型，并部署到多端平台。

Result: 实验结果表明系统检测有效率达92-95%，在不同环境和设备平台上均表现出一致的性能。

Conclusion: 该系统提高了病害检测效率，降低了对人工检查的依赖，实现了及时干预，支持可持续的互联精准农业农场。

Abstract: Plant diseases cause significant yield losses worldwide, with tomato crops particularly susceptible to early blight, late blight, and leaf mold. Manual monitoring is practical only for small-scale farms and becomes unmanageable at larger scales. To tackle this limitation, an artificial intelligence (AI) enabled cloud-edge architecture is proposed for autonomous crop monitoring. This proposed architecture integrates Internet of Things (IoT) sensors, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), deep learning, Azure IoT Hub-based cloud analytics, and multi-platform (mobile app, web app, and embedded edge device platform) interfaces to enable real-time detection of tomato diseases. For training and validation, we used publicly available datasets, such as PlantVillage and Kaggle. A TensorFlow model trained on a collected dataset is deployed across mobile, web, and edge-device platforms. Experimental results show detection effectiveness around 92-95%, with consistent performance over diverse environments and device platforms. The proposed system improves disease detection effectiveness, lowers dependence on manual inspection, and enables prompt interventions, thereby supporting sustainable, connected precision agriculture farms.

</details>


### [168] [Designing Social Robots for Inclusive Child Wellbeing Assessment: Insights from Communities Supporting Developmental Language Disorder and Forced Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03820)
*Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Yue Lou,Alva Markelius,Emma Geijer-Simpson,Gustaf Gredebäck,Tamsin Jane Ford,Ginevra Castellano,Hatice Gunes,Georgina Warner,Jenny L. Gibson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究针对有沟通障碍的儿童（如发展性语言障碍儿童和被迫移民背景儿童）在心理健康评估中面临的困难，通过设计儿童-机器人互动活动作为设计探针，并与相关家长和专业人士进行焦点小组讨论，提出了一套具有伦理性和包容性的机器人介导幸福感评估设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自我报告问卷对儿童的语言理解和表达能力要求较高，难以适用于有沟通障碍的儿童（如发展性语言障碍和被迫移民背景儿童）。社会机器人有望支持幸福感评估，但缺乏关于如何为不同沟通需求的儿童设计包容、适宜且伦理可接受的互动的研究。

Method: 创建候选儿童-机器人互动活动作为设计探针，组织支持发展性语言障碍儿童和被迫移民背景儿童的家长及专业人士进行焦点小组讨论，并通过主题分析提取设计考量因素。

Result: 识别出与机器人角色与能力、互动动态、个体差异和儿童能动性相关的考量因素，以及受儿童沟通需求和生活经历影响的人群特定考量。基于这些发现，推导出一套伦理且包容的机器人介导幸福感评估设计建议。

Conclusion: 本研究为面向不同沟通需求儿童的包容性机器人介导幸福感评估提供了设计指导，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Abstract: Assessing children's wellbeing and mental health can be particularly challenging for children experiencing communication barriers, such as children with Developmental Language Disorder (DLD) and children with forced migration backgrounds. During the assessment process, traditional self-report questionnaires place substantial demands on language comprehension and verbal expression. In this context, social robots have emerged as a promising tool for supporting wellbeing assessment without solely relying on self-report questionnaires, yet limited research has examined how such interactions can be designed to be inclusive, appropriate, and ethically acceptable for children with diverse communication needs. To address this gap, we created candidate child--robot interaction activities as design probes and conducted focus groups with parents and professionals supporting children with DLD and children with forced migration backgrounds. Through thematic analysis, we identified considerations relating to robot role and capabilities, interactional dynamics, individual differences, and child agency, alongside population-specific considerations shaped by children's communication needs and lived experiences. Based on these findings, we derive a set of ethical and inclusive design recommendations for robot-mediated wellbeing assessment. By foregrounding these considerations and recommendations, this work contributes design guidance for inclusive robot-mediated wellbeing assessments for children with diverse communication needs.

</details>


### [169] [EvoHIL: Self-Evolving Reward and Flow-Matched Policy Optimization for Robust Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03872)
*Shuoqin Zhang,Tongtong Cheng,Xiru Gao,Jinzhuo Peng,Bin Zheng,Jiahao Tu,Ke Wang,Jia Pan,Zhe Hu,Kai Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoHIL是一个统一框架，通过自进化奖励模型、动作流稳定和保留感知的离线微调三个模块，在人类参与的环境中适应奖励模型、动作生成器和视觉域变化，解决人类在环强化学习在接触丰富操作任务中面临的静态视觉奖励失效、动作时序不一致和视觉域偏移敏感三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在环强化学习使机器人能够从有限的真实交互中学习接触丰富的操作任务，但实际部署面临三个耦合的局限：静态视觉奖励模型在场景变化时失效、独立采样的动作导致时序不一致的运动、基于视觉的策略对外观变化敏感。

Method: 提出EvoHIL统一框架，包含三个核心模块：(1)自进化奖励（SER）从人类确认的正样本和临时弱负样本中自适应成功分类器；(2)动作流稳定（AFS）通过流匹配生成时序一致的动作块，将策略更新锚定在已执行的动作前缀和示范行为上；(3)保留感知的离线微调重放重新光照的交互数据，同时将AFS演员-评论家锚定到先前行为，无需额外的机器人交互即可适应视觉域。

Result: 在Franka FR3和SO-101机械臂的六个操作任务上，在受控光照变化条件下，EvoHIL相比人类在环和模仿学习基线，在任务成功率、与人工确认标签的一致性、运动平滑度和完成时间方面均有提升。

Conclusion: EvoHIL通过统一且分阶段的人类在环学习流程，同时自适应奖励模型、动作生成器和视觉域，有效解决了HIL-RL在接触丰富操作中的三类耦合局限，实现了更好的任务表现。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HIL-RL) enables robots to learn contact-rich manipulation from limited real-world interaction, but deployment exposes three coupled limitations: static visual reward models fail under scene changes; independently sampled actions cause temporally inconsistent motion; and vision-based policies remain sensitive to appearance shifts. We present EvoHIL, a unified framework that adapts the reward model, action generator, and visual do main within a staged human-in-the-loop learning process. First, self-evolving reward (SER) adapts the success classifier from human-confirmed positives and provisional weak negatives. Second, Action Flow Stabilization (AFS) generates temporally coherent action chunks through flow matching, grounding policy updates in executed action prefixes and demonstrated behavior. Third, retention-aware offline fine-tuning replays relit interaction data while anchoring the AFS actor-critic to prior behavior, adapting the visual domain without additional robot interaction. Across six manipulation tasks on Franka FR3 and SO-101 arms under a controlled lighting shift, EvoHIL improves task success, agreement with human-confirmation labels, motion smoothness, and completion time relative to human-in-the-loop and imitation baselines.Project page: https://anonymous4366.github.io/EvoHIL/

</details>


### [170] [ETA: A New Agentic Paradigm for Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03924)
*Yitong Chen,Zezheng Huai,Sixian Li,Yubang Wang,Haozhe Zhang,Yifei Zhang,Hechang Chen,Jingjing Gong,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了"具身任务代理"（Embodied Task Agent, ETA）这一新范式，将数字智能体扩展到物理世界，并发布了其开源实现OpenETA。ETA通过Planner-Interface-World的循环架构，实现规划、执行、验证和反思，使机器人能够在陌生环境中处理陌生任务，并具备可审计的记忆和可复现轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端机器人体统在泛化能力上严重依赖训练数据的覆盖范围，且长任务的执行难以控制和检查，距离实现机器人的"ChatGPT时刻"（即通用机器人）仍有很大差距。作者希望通过新的范式来解决这些问题。

Method: ETA采用中心化规划器的架构：Planner每次选择一个工具调用，Interface控制执行，World返回结果和新的观测。该循环使智能体能够验证结果、调整计划，并将成功和失败的交互转化为可复用的经验。OpenETA提供可替换的Planner、可组合的工具和技能、可审计的记忆、可回放的轨迹，以及面向仿真和真实机器人的通用接口。对于Codex，OpenETA可作为轻量插件，仅暴露observe、mark_point和move_to三个操作。

Result: 论文提出了OpenETA作为开源实现，展示了ETA范式如何将大语言模型（如Codex）与物理世界连接，支持以极简接口（仅三个操作）实现具身任务执行，并强调其可替换性、可组合性和可审计性等特性。

Conclusion: ETA范式通过结构化的人机协作循环（规划-执行-验证-反思）替代难以控制的端到端方法，为通用机器人的实现提供了一条可行路径，使机器人能够从经验中学习并保持长期可控性。

Abstract: When will robots have their ChatGPT moment? Such a breakthrough requires a general-purpose robot that can handle unfamiliar tasks in unfamiliar environments, remain controllable over long interactions, and learn from experience.
  Today's embodied systems largely follow an end-to-end observation-to-action path. Despite rapid progress, they remain far from this goal: their generalization depends heavily on the coverage of robot training data, while long task execution remains difficult to control and inspect. To realize this goal, we introduce the Embodied Task Agent (ETA), a new paradigm for extending digital agents into the physical world, and release OpenETA as its open-source implementation. ETA centers the robot around a Planner that chooses one Tool call at a time, an Interface that controls execution, and a World that returns the result and a fresh observation. This loop allows the agent to verify outcomes, adapt its plan, and turn successful and failed interactions into reusable experience. OpenETA provides replaceable Planners, composable Tools and Skills, auditable memory, replayable trajectories, and common interfaces for simulation and real robots. For Codex, OpenETA can operate as a lightweight plugin that exposes only observe, mark_point, and move_to.

</details>


### [171] [Bimanual Manipulation Within an 8 GB Budget: Zero-Copy Sensing and Quantized ACT on an Entry-Level Jetson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03938)
*Ekansh Singh,Eva Samuel,Alessandra Reneau,Ryan Schmeelk,Yashvi Gandhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano Super（入门级嵌入式GPU）上完整部署双臂SO-101机器人系统的方案，使用RTX 3070仅用于离线训练。通过GStreamer/NVMM零拷贝感知管线优化CPU利用率与延迟，对比ACT与Diffusion Policy的训练收敛差异，并将ACT转换为TensorRT（FP16提升6.4倍，INT8提升9.0倍），发现INT8量化仅适配ResNet18骨干而拒绝全部145个Transformer层，且量化需求取决于ACT的action-chunking配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作策略通常在高性能工作站或数据中心级GPU上评估，其在嵌入式硬件上的部署成本缺乏系统研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索在入门级嵌入式平台上运行双臂操作策略的可行性。

Method: 1）构建基于NVMM缓冲区的GStreamer三摄像头采集管线，对比传统路径与零拷贝路径的性能差异。2）在相同演示数据上分别训练ACT（100k梯度步）和Diffusion Policy（200k梯度步），评估收敛性。3）将ACT转换为TensorRT，分别使用FP16和INT8精度进行推理优化，并分析量化层分布。

Result: 零拷贝感知将峰值单核利用率从98.0%降至77.0%，最差延迟从117.31ms降至101.52ms。ACT收敛于19/20任务成功率，而Diffusion Policy在2倍步数下仍无法收敛（0/10）。TensorRT转换后FP16推理延迟降至17.93ms（6.4x），INT8降至12.65ms（9.0x），三种精度均保持任务成功率。INT8量化仅量化ResNet18骨干，拒绝全部145个Transformer层。

Conclusion: 嵌入式平台（Jetson Orin Nano Super）足以支持完整双臂操作策略部署，前提是采用合适的算法（ACT优于Diffusion Policy）和推理优化（TensorRT）。研究还揭示了ACT在TensorRT INT8量化中的两个新发现：量化层分布特性与action-chunking配置相关的量化条件性需求。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation policies trained with imitation learning are typically evaluated on workstation or datacenter-class GPUs, leaving the cost of deploying them on embedded hardware largely uncharacterized. We present a bimanual SO-101 system running entirely on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano Super (8 GB), the entry-level tier of NVIDIA's embedded line, using a desktop GPU (RTX 3070) only for offline training, evaluated on pick-and-place of a deformable beanbag. First, we build a GStreamer capture pipeline backed by NVMM buffers that removes redundant host-device copies from three-camera sensing. Contrary to expectation, the conventional path fit the memory budget and dropped no frames; what zero-copy sensing recovers is CPU headroom (peak single-core utilization 98.0% to 77.0%) and worst-case latency (117.31 ms to 101.52 ms). Second, we train ACT and Diffusion Policy on identical demonstrations, each at its own reference budget (100k gradient steps for ACT, 200k for Diffusion Policy). ACT converges to a task-competent policy (19/20 trials) while Diffusion Policy does not converge to a usable one (0/10) even at twice the step count, which we attribute to differing convergence costs rather than an accuracy ceiling. Third, we convert ACT to TensorRT. FP16 reduces mean inference latency from 114.02 ms to 17.93 ms (6.4x) and INT8 to 12.65 ms (9.0x), with task success preserved at all three precisions (19/20, 18/20, 19/20). We report two findings not previously documented for ACT: TensorRT's general-purpose INT8 calibration quantizes the ResNet18 backbone but accepts zero of 145 transformer layers, explaining INT8's negligible size reduction over FP16 (0.9%) despite a further 28% latency gain; and the need for quantization is conditional on ACT's action-chunking configuration, feasible in full precision at n_action_steps = 100 but not at the per-step re-prediction temporal ensembling requires.

</details>


### [172] [Stochastic Multiple Shooting Trajectory Optimization via Sequential Local Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03978)
*Ashwin Gupta,Joseph Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机多目发射击轨迹优化方法，通过将长动作序列优化问题分解为短控制序列并用局部反馈策略连接，提高了采样效率和终端集收敛速度。该方法还能仅从回放数据中合成近似系统雅可比矩阵，适用于黑盒动力学的模型强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机单目发射击轨迹优化方法（如MPPI）在机器人领域被广泛采用，但在长动作序列下射击时，终端约束的满足往往采样效率低下，需要大量迭代才能收敛。

Method: 提出一种随机多目发射击方法，优化由局部反馈策略连接的短控制动作序列，以提高采样效率和终端集收敛性。同时展示仅从回放数据中合成近似系统雅可比矩阵的能力，使方法适用于具有黑盒动力学的模型强化学习。

Result: 在三个非线性欠驱动优化问题中验证了改进的采样效率和终端集收敛性：具有解析动力学的经典cartpole摆动任务、具有学习神经网络动力学的cartpole摆动任务，以及VTOL四平面飞行器执行高攻角精确失速后着陆机动。

Conclusion: 随机多目发射击方法相比单目发射击方法在长序列优化中具有更好的采样效率和终端约束收敛性能，并且无需显式模型梯度即可工作，适用于广泛的黑盒动力学场景。

Abstract: Stochastic single shooting trajectory optimization methods such as Model Predictive Path Integral control (MPPI) have been widely adopted in robotics due to their ability to reason about probabilistic dynamics and provide solutions where model gradients are noisy, costly to evaluate, or unavailable. However, satisfaction of terminal constraints when shooting over long action sequences is often sample inefficient, requiring a large number of iterations for convergence. In this paper, we present a stochastic multiple shooting method that optimizes short control action sequences connected via local feedback policies to improve sample efficiency and convergence to a terminal set. Additionally, we show that we are able to synthesize approximate system Jacobians purely from rollouts, making the method suitable for model-based reinforcement learning with black-box dynamics. We demonstrate the algorithm has improved sample efficiency and terminal set convergence for three nonlinear, underactuated optimization problems: a classic cartpole swingup task with analytical dynamics, a cartpole swingup task with learned neural network dynamics, and a VTOL quadplane performing a high angle-of-attack, precision post-stall landing maneuver.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Deep Divide-and-Reduce in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02628)
*Yusong Deng,Yanjie Li,Weijun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DDRSR方法，通过严格的数学推导扩展了符号回归中表达式分解与约简的适用范围，避免了AI Feynman方法中依赖暴力搜索子表达式的局限，在表达式分解和数值回归任务上均展现了显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归的机器学习方法缺乏对表达式内在数学和物理原理的深入理解。AI Feynman方法虽然利用了数据的数学性质，但其表达式简化机制适用范围窄，容易在复杂方程上失败，且依赖暴力搜索子表达式，严重限制了实际应用。

Method: 通过严格的数学推导和证明，提出Deep Divide and Reduce in Symbolic Regression（DDRSR）方法。该方法从根本上拓宽了表达式分解与约简的适用范围，避免了暴力子结构搜索，同时保证了更广泛的通用性和严格的理论正确性。

Result: 实证评估表明，DDRSR在表达式分解和数值回归任务上均取得了显著优势，验证了其理论原则的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了该范式的适用场景和内在局限性，并指出了未来研究中有前景的方向。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is the task of discovering underlying patterns from data and representing them using mathematical expressions. Current machine learning approaches to SR often lack a profound understanding of the intrinsic mathematical and physical principles governing these expressions. While the pioneering AI Feynman method leverages the mathematical properties underlying the data, its expression simplification mechanism suffers from a narrow scope of applicability and is prone to failure on complex equations. Furthermore, its underlying mechanisms rely heavily on brute-force searches for sub-expressions, severely limiting its practical utility. Through rigorous mathematical deduction and proofs, we propose our method, Deep Divide and Reduce in Symbolic Regression (DDRSR). DDRSR fundamentally broadens the applicability of expression decomposition and reduction, circumvents the need for brute-force sub-structure searches, and ensures both wider versatility and strict theoretical correctness. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that these theoretical principles yield significant advantages in both expression decomposition and numerical regression tasks. Finally, we discuss the applicable scenarios and inherent limitations of this paradigm, alongside promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [174] [Multimodal Auto-regressive Transformer Surrogate for Modeling Variable Operations and Quantifying Uncertainty in Geological Carbon Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02629)
*Yifu Han,Louis J. Durlofsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多模态自回归Transformer代理模型，用于在地质不确定性下模拟CO2地质封存操作（含可变化井射孔和注入策略），并在层次化MCMC数据同化中实现了显著的不确定性缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变井射孔和注入策略可提高CO2地质封存效率，但现有方法难以高效地在多模态数据（3D地质模型、相对渗透率参数、控制变量）下建模这些操作并处理地质不确定性，需要一种更高效的代理模型。

Method: 构建多模态自回归Transformer代理模型：三个输入模态（3D地质模型、标量渗透率参数、控制变量）分别通过独立编码器处理，经Transformer编码器中的自注意力融合，再由时间解码器通过编码器-解码器交叉注意力自回归生成预测。训练使用4000次GEOS流动模拟。

Result: 在新测试集上饱和度的中位MAE为0.028，其他关注量的中位相对误差为0.2-5%，并能捕捉速率到井底压力控制的切换。在层次化MCMC数据同化中显著降低关键元参数（尤其是断层渗透率）的不确定性，后验预测与真实模型结果总体一致。

Conclusion: 该多模态自回归Transformer代理模型能高效准确地模拟CO2封存操作并支持不确定性量化，在数据同化框架中表现出色，为地质碳封存的全流程优化与风险评估提供了有力工具。

Abstract: The use of variable well perforation and injection strategies can improve the efficiency of geological carbon storage operations. We develop a new multimodal auto-regressive transformer surrogate to model these operations under geological uncertainty. A modified SEAM CO2 geomodel, which involves a faulted system with three stacked aquifers, is considered. The two injection wells are perforated in stages, from bottom to top, with the stage durations and individual well injection rates treated as control variables. The surrogate model processes three input modalities - the 3D geomodel, scalar parameters characterizing relative permeability functions, and control variables - through separate encoders. These are fused via self-attention in a transformer encoder, and a temporal decoder generates predictions auto-regressively through encoder-decoder cross-attention. The surrogate is trained, using 4000 GEOS flow simulations, to predict saturation and pressure at monitoring locations, total injected and mobile CO2 mass, and saturation footprints. For a new test set, involving randomly sampled geomodels and control variables, the surrogate achieves a median saturation MAE of 0.028 and median relative errors of 0.2-5% for the other quantities of interest. Importantly, it captures the switch from rate to bottom-hole-pressure control. The surrogate model is used within a hierarchical Markov chain Monte Carlo data assimilation procedure for a synthetic true model under three operational strategies. Substantial uncertainty reduction is achieved for key metaparameters, particularly the fault permeabilities. Posterior predictions for saturation footprints and total injected and mobile CO2 mass are also shown to be generally consistent with true model results.

</details>


### [175] [LLMs Can Annotate Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02632)
*Ameen Patel,Max Zhang,Nathan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化电路追踪流水线，通过直接向语言模型呈现特征描述来将个体特征或MLP神经元分组为超节点，无需耗时的人工标注步骤。实验表明该流水线生成的超节点与人工标注的同样可解释，并在两跳Capitals任务中97/100的提示词成功恢复中间跳对应的超节点，还展示了在开放式探索中的概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路追踪是揭示语言模型内部计算的有效技术，但现有的超节点分组步骤需要耗时的人工干预，严重限制了该技术的可扩展性和广泛应用。

Method: 提出一个简单的自动化流水线：将特征或MLP神经元的描述直接呈现给一个语言模型，由其对特征进行自动分组生成超节点。使用自动化可解释性指标验证超节点质量，并在两跳Capitals任务和Wikipedia提示词完成的归因图上进行实验。

Result: 该流水线生成的超节点与人工标注的超节点具有同等的可解释性；在两跳Capitals任务中，100个提示词中有97个成功恢复了对应中间跳的超节点；并成功自动标注了1000个来自Wikipedia提示词完成的归因图，用LLM判断器标记了值得人工审查的有趣图。

Conclusion: 简单自动化也能产生有意义的归因图标注，为自动化电路追踪的未来研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Circuit tracing is an exciting technique for revealing the internal computation of language models, but it requires a time-intensive manual step of grouping individual features or MLP neurons into supernodes. We present a simple pipeline for automating this step: directly presenting feature descriptions to a language model that groups them into supernodes. Using automated interpretability metrics, we confirm that supernodes generated by our pipeline are as interpretable as those generated by human annotators. On a two-hop Capitals task, our pipeline recovers a supernode corresponding to the intermediate hop in 97 of 100 prompts. Finally, we present a simple proof of concept using our pipeline for open-ended exploration, where we automatically annotate 1000 attribution graphs from Wikipedia prompt completions and then use an LLM judge to flag interesting graphs worth human review. We hope this work demonstrates that even simple automation can produce meaningful attribution graph annotations, motivating further work on automated circuit tracing.

</details>


### [176] [GeoID-PINN: Identifiability-Aware Regional Epidemic Inference with Geographic Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02633)
*Weixiong Hua,Fan Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoID-PINN，一种用于易感-感染-恢复-死亡（SIRD）动力学的物理信息神经网络，通过行随机源组成矩阵建模空间依赖关系，并利用距离、邻接、通勤或领先-滞后信息的空间先验进行正则化。在四区域模拟和路易斯安那州64县COVID-19数据的评估中，模型在轨迹拟合和点预测准确性上表现良好，但揭示出准确轨迹并不保证区域依赖结构的正确恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域监测数据混合了本地传播、报告延迟、种子引入和外部感染压力等多种因素，这些因素难以单独识别。现有方法无法有效区分区域间的依赖结构，需要一种能够同时建模空间依赖和疾病传播动态的方法。

Method: 提出GeoID-PINN框架：1) 基于SIRD动力学构建物理信息神经网络；2) 使用行随机源组成矩阵表示空间依赖，每行分配非负且和为1的源权重；3) 通过距离、邻接、通勤或领先-滞后信息构建空间先验对矩阵进行正则化；4) 在模拟数据和真实COVID-19数据上进行验证，与自回归负二项基线对比。

Result: 四区域模拟中，兼容距离先验的源组成误差为0.099，无正则化时为0.159，强错误先验时为0.577。路易斯安那州64县COVID-19预测中，MSE从32,957降至11,468，MAE从70.60降至57.73，但基线在NLL上更优（5.158 vs 5.346）。15县对比中，邻接关系使MSE降低6.85%，MAE降低3.1%。

Conclusion: 准确预测轨迹不能保证区域依赖结构的准确恢复；结构化正则化能改进点预测准确性，但无法唯一确定边的恢复；模型解释需要先验敏感性和观测模型检查。

Abstract: Regional surveillance data reflect local transmission, reporting, seeding, and external infection pressure, which are difficult to identify separately. We introduce GeoID-PINN, a physics-informed neural network (PINN) for susceptible-infectious-recovered-deceased (SIRD) dynamics. The model represents spatial dependence with a row-stochastic source-composition matrix whose rows assign nonnegative source weights that sum to one. We regularize this matrix toward a spatial prior constructed from distance, adjacency, commuting, or lead-lag information. In a four-region simulation with known truth, a compatible distance prior gives source-composition error 0.099. The error rises to 0.159 without regularization and 0.577 under a strongly misspecified prior, while trajectory fit and transmission-scale estimates remain similar. Accurate trajectories therefore do not guarantee recovery of the regional dependence structure. We also evaluate GeoID-PINN retrospectively using COVID-19 data from 64 Louisiana counties. Relative to an autoregressive negative-binomial baseline, Forecast-Trained Geo-PINN reduces mean squared error (MSE) from 32,957 to 11,468 and mean absolute error (MAE) from 70.60 to 57.73. The baseline has lower negative log likelihood (NLL), 5.158 versus 5.346, indicating better distributional fit but worse point accuracy. In a controlled 15-county comparison, county adjacency reduces MSE by 6.85 percent and MAE by 3.1 percent. Similar performance across plausible priors supports structured regularization but not unique edge recovery. These results require prior-sensitivity and observation-model checks before interpretation.

</details>


### [177] [Verifier-Guided Model Discovery for Physical Dynamical Systems with Pretrained Symbolic Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02662)
*Farbod Faraji,Francesco Belardinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种验证器引导（VG）的神经到符号工作流，以ODEFormer为符号骨干，利用动力学和物理可接受性准则从多轨迹候选方程池中选择方程，实现从合成ODE轨迹到高维物理数据的可靠迁移。在Van der Pol振荡器和涡脱落（固定及变化雷诺数）上验证了该方法的有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真物理仿真成本高昂，机器学习代理模型不透明且编码了系统动力学假设，限制了泛化能力。预训练的基于合成ODE轨迹到方程映射的Transformer模型虽可解释且无需系统特定方程知识，但可靠迁移到高维物理数据仍是一个开放性挑战。

Method: 围绕ODEFormer作为符号骨干，开发验证器引导（VG）工作流，利用动力学和物理可接受性准则从多轨迹候选方程池中进行筛选，通过坐标降维和符号发现来处理固定及变化雷诺数下的涡脱落现象。

Result: VG在Van der Pol振荡器的未见过初始条件上优于原始ODEFormer工作流。在涡脱落任务中，VG发现了固定参数的降阶方程，恢复基础脱落振荡器和高次谐波，无需尾流特定候选库或预设NS结构；跨参数模型可泛化到未见的流态。重建保真度本身不能决定符号可发现性，揭示了潜在动力学与骨干预训练分布的兼容性重要性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了验证器引导的神经到符号方法论，为自然科学中可解释且物理可审计的预测提供了新途径，强调了潜在动力学与预训练分布兼容性的关键作用。

Abstract: Reliable forecasting of nonlinear physical systems underpins scientific discovery and engineering decision-making. Yet high-fidelity simulations are prohibitively costly, and machine-learning surrogates can be opaque and encode assumptions about system dynamics, limiting generalizability. Pretrained transformers mapping synthetic ODE trajectories to equations offer interpretable alternatives, promising transfer without system-specific equation knowledge. Transferring them reliably to high-dimensional physical data, however, remains an open challenge. We develop a verifier-guided (VG) workflow around ODEFormer as a symbolic backbone, using dynamical and physical-admissibility criteria to select from a multi-trajectory candidate equation pool, enabling transfer. On canonical Van der Pol oscillators, VG outperforms the original ODEFormer workflow across held-out initial conditions. We then address vortex shedding, a phenomenon occurring in atmospheric and plasma systems of societal relevance, through coordinate reduction and symbolic discovery at fixed and varying Reynolds numbers.
